- `s2` [partnership, 2026-05] KUKA and Visual Components announced joint development of simulation-driven automation solutions spanning planning, virtual commissioning and operations.  
  > "Together with Visual Components, KUKA Group is advancing the development of simulation-driven solutions" ([source](https://www.kuka.com/en-us/company/press/news/2026/05/visual-components-simulation-driven-automation))
- `s3` [growth, 2025-11] Visual Components opened a Japanese subsidiary in Tokyo and runs a joint venture with Mitsubishi Electric in Aichi Prefecture; the company has over 130 employees and over 2,400 global customers.  
  > "Visual Components has over 130 employees. It is a company specializing in manufacturing simulation software" ([source](https://note.com/kmoriyama/n/nb3030943f050?hl=en))
- `s4` [partnership, 2026-01] Visual Components launched the VC Ecosystem, a section for partner add-ons and plugins, with an ergonomics integration from Work(s) Ergo as the first featured add-on.  
  > "The new VC Ecosystem aims to promote partner-developed technologies, add-ons and plugins" ([source](https://www.visualcomponents.com/press/visual-components-announces-the-vc-ecosystem-and-partnership-with-worksergo-to-bring-advanced-ergonomics-planning-to-the-simulation-platform/))
- `s5` [customers, 2026-01] MSK Finland reported that using Visual Components offline programming and factory simulation cut typical robot programming time by around 50%.  
  > "typical programming time has been reduced by around 50%, depending on cell complexity" ([source](https://www.automation-mag.com/application-stories/105367-msk-finland-improves-efficiency-with-olp-and-factory-simulation))
- `s6` [technology, 2026-06] Visual Components 5.1 upgrades its physics engine to PhysX 5.5 and adds support for Allen-Bradley PLCs.  
  > "Visual Components 5.1 upgrades the physics engine to PhysX 5.5." ([source](https://www.visualcomponents.com/blog/introducing-visual-components-5-1-orchestrate-the-floor-with-ease/))
- `s7` [hiring] Visual Components' careers page describes the company as part of KUKA Group with around 120 employees from 20 nationalities and says it continually seeks talented people.  
  > "We’re constantly looking for motivated and talented individuals to join our team" ([source](https://www.visualcomponents.com/careers/))

- **Launched simulation platform version 5.1** (2026-06; s1, s6): Adds support for hundreds of AMRs and AGVs, up to 10x faster performance, PhysX 5.5 physics and Allen-Bradley PLCs.

- **Customer cut robot programming time about 50%** (2026-01; s5): MSK Finland reported that offline programming and factory simulation cut typical robot programming time by around 50%.
- **Launched VC Ecosystem for partner add-ons** (2026-01; s4): A new section for partner add-ons and plugins, with an ergonomics integration from Work(s) Ergo as the first featured add-on.

**Likely needs (inferred): growing**

Its simulation platform now handles hundreds of AMRs and AGVs, with up to 10x faster performance.

- **Senior C# and C++ software engineers** (Senior; C#, C++, Azure DevOps, Git, Visual Studio): Version 5.1 and the KUKA partnership expand a large desktop simulation product that needs maintained and extended native code. (s1, s2, hiring now: C#/C++ Senior Software Engineer)
  - can do: Modernize legacy monolithic desktop software into maintainable modules
  - can do: Design algorithms and architecture for large-scale simulation
  - can do: Own build, dependency and CI/CD pipelines in Azure DevOps
- **Frontend engineers for browser 3D tools** (Experienced; TypeScript, React, SignalR, Playwright, pnpm): The VC Ecosystem launch and partner add-ons call for web interfaces that plug into the platform. (s4, hiring now: Senior Frontend Software Developer)
  - can do: Build browser-based 3D visualization interfaces with React and TypeScript
  - can do: Publish reusable npm packages for partner add-ons
  - can do: Design REST and WebSocket APIs for real-time simulation data
- **Engineering leads for product infrastructure** (Lead; Scrum, C#, C++, Build management, Agile): Modernizing the legacy codebase while growing the team requires someone to lead the product infrastructure group. (s1, s3, hiring now: Engineering Lead - Product Infra team)
  - can do: Lead a software team through a legacy modernization program
  - can do: Run Agile and Scrum delivery across build and release work
- **Industrial controls QA engineers** (Early career; CODESYS, IEC 61131-3, IEC 61499, OPC UA, PLC programming): Support for Allen-Bradley PLCs and OPC UA-based virtual commissioning needs testers who can validate control logic. (s6, s2, hiring now: Junior Software QA Engineer)
  - can do: Test PLC programs written in FBD, ST and SFC
  - can do: Verify virtual commissioning against OPC UA interfaces

## Zero Gravity

https://zerogravity.fi · not in our Espoo job data

<img src="https://media.brand.dev/5d25594d-4960-48ec-9226-72da937006cd.png" height="28"> Guaranteeing high-resolution thermal data where heat sources matter most. · Aerospace & Defense: Space & Satellite Technology · 1 to 10 employees (identity: Context.dev)

**Stated in public sources**

- `s1` [funding, 2025-12] Zero Gravity Oy was selected for the ESA Phi-Lab Finland program, which will support commercialising its research over the next two years.  
  > "Over the next two years, they will commercialise their research through different projects in Earth observation" ([source](https://startupcenter.aalto.fi/esa-phi-lab-finland-welcomes-its-2-new-startups))
- `s2` [partnership, 2025-06] Zero Gravity Oy was named one of 20 winners of the SPACE4Cities tender, out of 141 applications, with funding of up to €36,000 for a detailed plan and up to €376,000 overall.  
  > "Jokainen voittaja saa enintään 36 000 euron rahoituksen yksityiskohtaisen toteutussuunnitelman luomiseen" ([source](https://forumvirium.fi/release/eurooppalaisen-satelliittidatakilpailun-voittajat-valittu-mukana-suomalaiset-avoin-map-oy-ja-zero-gravity-oy/))
- `s3` [partnership, 2026-04] Zero Gravity Oy was selected to join the Cassini Business Accelerator Batch 6, run by the European Commission and EUSPA.  
  > "We are proud to announce that Zero Gravity Oy has been selected to join the Cassini Business Accelerator Batch 6" ([source](https://www.zerogravity.fi/%f0%9f%9a%80-zero-gravity-oy-joins-the-cassini-business-accelerator-batch-6/))
- `s4` [funding] Zero Gravity Oy received ESA Phi-Lab Finland funding to develop a thermal imaging payload demonstrator for CubeSats.  
  > "Zero Gravity Oy has received funding from the ESA Phi-Lab Finland!" ([source](https://www.zerogravity.fi/zero-gravity-oy-receives-esa-phi-lab-finland-funding-to-advance-next-generation-thermal-imaging/))
- `s5` [product] Zero Gravity Oy is developing a miniaturized thermal imaging payload for CubeSats that aims to deliver resolution far finer than existing ~1 km thermal satellite systems.  
  > "Current thermal Earth Observation systems operate at ~1 km resolution" ([source](https://www.zerogravity.fi/))
- `s6` [customers] Zero Gravity Oy has paying clients in renewable energy, smart city and water monitoring across Europe, and its UrbanAI platform has been offered free to the ecosystem under an ESA contract.  
  > "We are commercial / have paying clients" ([source](https://www.zerogravity.fi/))

**What to tell candidates**

- **Selected for ESA Phi-Lab Finland program** (2025-12; s1): Chosen to commercialise its research over the next two years, following earlier ESA Phi-Lab funding for a thermal payload demonstrator.
- **Joined Cassini Business Accelerator Batch 6** (2026-04; s3): Selected by the European Commission and EUSPA to join the accelerator, which supports space-based business growth.
- **Paying clients across Europe in three sectors** (2026-11; s6): Serves renewable energy, smart city and water monitoring customers, and offered its UrbanAI platform free to the ecosystem under an ESA contract.
- **Building a fine-resolution CubeSat thermal payload** (2026-11; s5, s4): Developing a miniaturized thermal imaging payload aimed at resolution far finer than existing roughly 1 km thermal satellite systems.

Building a CubeSat thermal camera with far finer resolution than today's satellites, for city and energy monitoring.

  - can do: Validate sensor performance under orbital thermal conditions
